The public export writes\n# MANIFEST.json at the repo root with \"target\": \"public\", so the tree itself says which\n# build it is: nobody building from the public repo has to remember an env var, and the\n# private monorepo (no such MANIFEST) keeps its historical `dogfood` default.\n#\n# An explicit PAPERCUSP_DISTRIBUTION_PROFILE still wins; callers apply that themselves.\n\n# default_distribution_profile <desktop-root>\ndefault_distribution_profile() {\n  local manifest=\"$1/../MANIFEST.json\"\n  if [[ -f \"$manifest\" ]] && grep -Eq '\"target\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"public\"' \"$manifest\"; then\n    echo public\n  else\n    echo dogfood\n  fi\n}\n"},{"path":"papercusp-desktop/bin/release.config.ts","repository":"papercusp-desktop","revision":"8dbe1613f64d555b733ab3a8cd6f8fcc1cd34ded","bytes":7519,"sha256":"948949f3e50c8778ccf1d31955c47533aa2b11fdffeaa4c67e3a124d14b4f4a4","selectedEvidence":[{"line":75,"text":"registerTargetDriver(linuxX86Driver);"},{"line":76,"text":"registerTargetDriver(linuxArm64Driver);"},{"line":84,"text":"'windows-x86_64',"},{"line":99,"text":"'macos-universal',"},{"line":131,"text":"id: Every source lead needs an independently justified applicability/disposition; test/retired roles are never automatically notApplicable.","sourceRows":[{"path":"libs/generic/plugin-loader/src/loader.ts","repository":".","repositoryRevision":"42d905b5b2fcbea2bb651178a7ff3c905cf89626","sha256":"2286cf5876ed0b3651d338e6e3479b333c5f5a3ab347b4aed11f45e6d81c8643","bytes":10896,"excerpts":[{"startLine":180,"endLine":223,"text":"  async function loadFromDir(\n    dir: string,\n    source: string,\n  ): Promise<LoadFromDirResult<TPlugin, TManifest>> {\n    const path = resolve(dir);\n    const manifest = await ports.readManifest(path);\n    // A directory with neither manifest nor entry point isn't a\n    // misconfigured plugin — it's not a plugin dir at all. Skip silently\n    // instead of polluting the error log.\n    if (!manifest) {\n      const entry = await ports.locateEntry(path);\n      if (!entry) return { skipped: 'not-a-plugin', path };\n      return { error: ports.invalidManifestError ?? 'missing or invalid manifest', path };\n    }\n    if (typeof manifest === 'object' && '__error' in manifest) {\n      return { error: (manifest as ManifestError).__error, path };\n    }\n\n    const excluded = ports.admitManifest?.(manifest as TManifest, source) ?? null;\n    if (excluded) return { skipped: 'excluded', path, reason: excluded };\n\n    // Alternate-runtime manifests (e.g. wasm/daemon) can short-circuit the\n    // JS entry/import/validate path entirely.\n    if (ports.runtimeDispatch) {\n      const dispatched = await ports.runtimeDispatch(manifest as TManifest, path, source);\n      if (dispatched) return dispatched;\n      // null → fall through to the JS path.\n    }\n\n    const entry = await ports.locateEntry(path);\n    if (!entry) return { error: ports.noEntryError ?? 'no entry point found', path };\n    const imported = await importEntry(entry);\n    if (!imported) return { error: `import returned null/non-object from ${entry}`, path };\n    if (typeof imported === 'object' && 'error' in imported) {\n      return { error: `import threw at ${entry}: ${(imported as { error: string }).error}`, path };\n    }\n    const plugin = imported as TPlugin;\n    if (ports.validatePlugin) {\n      const validationError = await ports.validatePlugin(plugin, manifest as TManifest);\n      if (validationError) return { error: validationError, path };\n    }\n    return { plugin, manifest: manifest as TManifest, path, source };\n  }\n"}]},{"path":"packages/plugin-loader/src/index.ts","repository":".","repositoryRevision":"42d905b5b2fcbea2bb651178a7ff3c905cf89626","sha256":"a953ed9a72218b4aae265dd0cf8cfe918e6a490b79ae3c7502936db01345df3d","bytes":48147,"excerpts":[{"startLine":573,"endLine":609,"text":"async function runtimeDispatch(\n  manifest: ManifestShape,\n  path: string,\n  source: string,\n): Promise<CoreLoadedPlugin<Plugin, ManifestShape> | { error: string; path: string } | null> {\n  const runtime = manifest.runtime;\n  // kind='pack' purity, runtime half (tool-distribution-granularity D-004):\n  // wasm/daemon manifests short-circuit validate() below, so the runtime-less\n  // rule for packs must gate HERE, before the dispatch.\n  if ((manifest as { kind?: string }).kind === 'pack' && runtime && runtime.kind !== 'js') {\n    return {\n      error: `pack \"${(manifest as { name?: string }).name ?? '?'}\" declares runtime.kind \"${runtime.kind}\" — a pack is runtime-less (js-loaded tool handlers only); use kind \"plugin\"`,\n      path,\n    };\n  }\n  if (runtime?.kind === 'wasm') {\n    if (!runtime.wasmPath) {\n      return { error: 'manifest.runtime.kind = \"wasm\" but runtime.wasmPath missing', path };\n    }\n    const wasmAbs = resolve(path, runtime.wasmPath);\n    try {\n      // Stat-only — actual instantiation happens at enable time so a\n      // browse over installed plugins doesn't pay the wasmtime startup\n      // cost. The shim records intent; the operator's plugin-host-runtime\n      // calls loadWasmPlugin() at enable.\n      await fs.stat(wasmAbs);\n    } catch {\n      return { error: `wasm artifact not found at ${wasmAbs}`, path };\n    }\n    return { plugin: makeWasmPluginShim(manifest, wasmAbs), manifest, path, source };\n  }\n  if (runtime?.kind === 'daemon') {\n    if (!runtime.daemonCommand || runtime.daemonCommand.length === 0) {\n      return { error: 'manifest.runtime.kind = \"daemon\" but runtime.daemonCommand missing/empty', path };\n    }\n    return { plugin: makeDaemonPluginShim(manifest), manifest, path, source };\n  }"},{"startLine":743,"endLine":767,"text":"export async function loadPlugins(opts: LoaderOptions = {}): Promise<{\n  loaded: LoadedPlugin[];\n  errors: Array<{ error: string; path: string }>;\n}> {\n  // Build the Papercusp search roots (precedence: project > harness >\n  // global). The generic kernel walks + dedupes them by plugin name\n  // (first-seen wins); the `~/.papercusp` root resolution + scope-recursion\n  // policy is the Papercusp-specific part that stays here.\n  const roots: DiscoverRoot[] = [];\n  if (opts.projectDir) {\n    roots.push({ pluginsDir: join(opts.projectDir, 'plugins'), source: 'project' });\n  }\n  for (const slug of opts.harnessSlugs ?? []) {\n    roots.push({\n      pluginsDir: join(papercuspRootResolved(), 'harnesses', slug, 'plugins'),\n      source: 'harness',\n    });\n  }\n  const globalDir = opts.globalPluginsDir ?? join(papercuspRootResolved(), 'global-plugins');\n  // Global plugins may live under npm-style `@scope/` dirs (e.g.\n  // `@papercupai/`); recurse those into per-plugin candidates.\n  roots.push({ pluginsDir: globalDir, source: 'global', scopeRecurse: true });\n\n  const { loaded, errors } = await corePluginLoader.loadAll(roots);\n  return { loaded: loaded.map(toLoadedPlugin), errors };"}]},{"path":"packages/plugin-loader/src/daemon/supervisor.ts","repository":".","repositoryRevision":"42d905b5b2fcbea2bb651178a7ff3c905cf89626","sha256":"c7d1fd5e58d6bf1b53ee0d41240cb4ecd29e36727a30beb0a0bc747b8b168e44","bytes":18412,"excerpts":[{"startLine":250,"endLine":292,"text":"      }\n      if (probe.transient) {\n        // An inconclusive probe is not a refusal (WI-6554700010018251: a timed-out\n        // probe on a loaded host used to leave the provider unregistered). Spawn\n        // through bwrap anyway: bwrap refuses to run the command without the\n        // profile, so the daemon still never runs unsandboxed, and a real failure\n        // surfaces as the daemon's exit.\n        opts.onLog?.(`[supervisor] ${opts.pluginName}: provider sandbox probe inconclusive (${probe.detail}); spawning through bwrap`);\n      }\n      const built = buildProviderBwrapArgs({\n        pluginDir: opts.cwd,\n        runtimeDirs: opts.runtimeDirs,\n        bwrapBinary: opts.bwrapBinary ?? * event-loop-sentinel.worker.ts — the sentinel body, running on a\n * `worker_threads` Worker so it keeps ticking while the MAIN event loop is\n * blocked. Started by `event-loop-sentinel-host.ts`; the decision itself lives\n * in the pure `event-loop-sentinel.ts`.\n *\n * ## Three measured facts this file is built on\n *\n * All three were verified empirically (2026-08-04, EI-19465075959589134) rather\n * than assumed, because each one silently breaks the feature if wrong.\n *\n * 1. **A worker keeps running while the main thread spins.** Probe: main thread\n *    blocked 3000 ms; the worker observed the freeze and decided at 600 ms of\n *    staleness. Had it been blocked too it would have reported ~3000 ms after\n *    the fact. This is the premise of the whole design.\n *\n * 2. **`console.log` from a worker DOES NOT ESCAPE a blocked main thread.** It\n *    is proxied through the parent port, so it queues behind the block — in the\n *    probe it printed only *after* the main loop was released. Since we SIGKILL,\n *    the process would die before it ever flushed: a mysterious death with no\n *    log line. `fs.writeSync(2, …)` goes straight to fd 2 and DID print during\n *    the block. **Never use console/`process.stderr.write` in this file.**\n *\n * 3. **This file may be `.ts`.** The repo convention for worker bodies is plain\n *    `.mjs` (`cpu-task-worker.script.mjs`) precisely to avoid a TypeScript\n *    transform in a worker. Measured: a Worker spawned from the tsx-run host\n *    (`npx tsx bin/hono-host.ts`) resolves and imports repo `.ts` fine. Staying\n *    in `.ts` lets this file import the SAME decider the tests exercise, which\n *    matters more here than convention — a hand-copied `.mjs` state machine\n *    could drift from the tested one, and a drifted copy of *this* logic\n *    SIGKILLs production. Spawn failure is handled fail-soft by the host, so a\n *    runtime where this does not load simply disables the sentinel."},{"startLine":45,"endLine":65,"text":"import { workerData, parentPort } from 'node:worker_threads';\nimport { Session } from 'node:inspector';\nimport { join } from 'node:path';\nimport {\n  writeSync,\n  readFileSync,\n  readlinkSync,\n  openSync,\n  readSync,\n  closeSync,\n  mkdirSync,\n  readdirSync,\n  renameSync,\n  rmSync,\n  writeFileSync,\n} from 'node:fs';\n// EXPLICIT `.ts` EXTENSION IS LOAD-BEARING (see the sentinel import below).\n// prettier-ignore\n// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/ban-ts-comment\n// @ts-ignore — the plain-node Worker requires an explicit .ts specifier; parseEmbedSidecarRecord(readFileSync(embedSidecarRecordPath(env), 'utf8'));","  } catch {","    return null;","  }","}","","/** Atomic 0600 write (tmp + rename) into a 0700 dir. A failure is logged, not"," *  thrown: this Server still uses the child it holds; only sibling adoption is lost. */","function writeEmbedSidecarRecord(rec: EmbedSidecarRecord, env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): void {","  const file = embedSidecarRecordPath(env);","  try {","    mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });","    const tmp = `${file}.${process.pid}.tmp`;","    writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(rec), { mode: 0o600 });","    renameSync(tmp, file);","  } catch (err) {","    console.warn('[embed-sidecar] could not record the sidecar address:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);","  }"]},{"start":300,"end":315,"lines":["}): EmbedSidecarAdoptVerdict {","  const { record, uid } = input;","  if (record.uid !== uid) return { adopt: false, reason: `recorded by uid ${record.uid}, this process is uid ${uid}` };","  if (!input.pidAlive) return { adopt: false, reason: `recorded pid ${record.pid} is not running as this uid` };","  if (input.listenerUids !== null && uid !== null) {","    if (input.listenerUids.length === 0) return { adopt: false, reason: `nothing listens on port ${record.port}` };","    if (!loopbackListenerOwnedBy(input.listenerUids, uid)) {","      return { adopt: false, reason: `port ${record.port} is held by uid(s) ${input.listenerUids.join(',')}` };","    }","  }","  if (input.healthPid !== undefined && input.healthPid !== record.pid) {","    return { adopt: false, reason: `/healthz on port ${record.port} reports pid ${input.healthPid}, not ${record.pid}` };","  }","  return { adopt: true };","}",""]},{"start":377,"end":393,"lines":["/** Fetch and parse /healthz at `url`, or null if nothing healthy answers."," *  Returns the PAYLOAD, not a boolean: liveness alone cannot decide whether a"," *  sidecar is usable, because a long-running one is frozen at its build time"," *  and 404s every route added since (EI-19314150478401738). Callers that need"," *  a route pair this with verifySidecarFitness(). */","export async function probeSidecarHealth(url: string, timeoutMs = 1500): Promise<unknown | null> {","  const ctl = new AbortController();","  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), timeoutMs);","  try {","    const res = await fetch(`${url.replace(/\\/$/, '')}/healthz`, { signal: ctl.signal });","    if (!res.ok) return null;","    return (await res.json()) as unknown;","  } catch {","    return null;","  } finally {","    clearTimeout(timer);","  }"]},{"start":527,"end":575,"lines":["","  // Spawn target. esbuild bundles EVERY module into the single serve.mjs, so in","  // a PACKAGED build import.meta.url here IS serve.mjs — re-exec that artifact","  // under the bundled node with PAPERCUSP_EMBED_SIDECAR_MODE=1 (serve.ts /","  // hono-host.ts divert to runEmbedSidecarServer). A mode-divert miss in","      // the re-exec'd entry otherwise boots a full host on the PARENT's port","      // (EADDRINUSE crash-respawn loop) and could double-run the single-writer","      // background machinery. Port 0 = ephemeral bind if some entry ever listens.","      PAPERCUSP_HONO_PORT: '0',","      PORT: '0',","      PAPERCUSP_BACKGROUND_WORKERS: '0',","    };","","    const child = spawn(plan.cmd, plan.args, {","      env,","      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe', 'ipc'],","      detached: false,","    });","    sidecarProcess = child;","    lastExitIdle = false;","    /** P-530: the child is gone — forget its port and drop its record. */","    const forgetChild = (): void => {","      spawnedPort = null;","      if (fixedPort === null && typeof child.pid === 'number') removeEmbedSidecarRecord(child.pid);"]},{"start":773,"end":805,"lines":[" * that instance instead of spawning a second child that would EADDRINUSE-die."," * A port held by another uid is refused, never adopted."," */","export async function ensureEmbedSidecar(","  requiredCapabilities: readonly string[] = [],","): Promise<string | null> {","  if (!embedSidecarEnabled()) {","    // WI-9354: gate on the opt-in under vitest only. EMBED_MAX_ATTEMPTS;","  const sleep = opts.sleep ?? realSleep;","  const totalBudgetMs = opts.maxTotalWaitMs ?? EMBED_TOTAL_BUDGET_MS;","  const markFailure = hooks.markFailure ?? (() => {});","  const admission = hooks.admission?.();","  return async (text: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<number[]> => {","    signal?.throwIfAborted();","    let lastErr: Error | null = null;","    let waitedMs = 0;","    const embedDeadlineAt = Date.now() + totalBudgetMs;","    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxAttempts; attempt++) {","      signal?.throwIfAborted();","      const remainingBudgetMs = embedDeadlineAt - Date.now();","      if (remainingBudgetMs <= 0) break;","      let serverHintMs: number | null = null;","      let slot: { release(): void } | null | undefined;","      try {","        slot = await admission?.acquire(text, 'memory');","      } catch (error) {","        if (hooks.isBudgetExhausted?.(error)) markFailure();","        throw error;","      }","      try {","        signal?.throwIfAborted();","        const fetchBudgetMs = embedDeadlineAt - Date.now();","        if (fetchBudgetMs <= 0) throw new Error('openai_embed_budget_spent');","        const response = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/embeddings', {","          method: 'POST',","          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}` },","          body: JSON.stringify({","            model: OPENAI_EMBEDDER_MODEL,","            input: text,","            dimensions: EMBEDDER_DIM_SPECS.openai.targetDims,","          }),","          signal: AbortSignal.any([","            ...(signal ? [signal] : []),","            AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(1, Math.min(EMBED_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS, fetchBudgetMs))),","          ]),","        });","        signal?.throwIfAborted();","        admission?.recordResponse(headersToRecord(response.headers));","        if (response.ok) {","          void hooks.clearAlert?.();","          const json = (await response.json()) as { data: Array<{ embedding: number[] }> };","          signal?.throwIfAborted();","          return json.data[0].embedding;","        }","        const body = await response.text().catch(() => '');","        if (response.status === 429 && /insufficient_quota/.test(body)) {","          markFailure(Date.now(), { hard: true });","          void hooks.maybeEscalate?.();","          throw new Error(`openai_embed_quota_exhausted: ${body}`);","        }","        const error = new Error(`openai_embed_failed_${response.status}: ${body}`);","        if (response.status !== 429 && response.status < 500) throw error;","        serverHintMs = retryDelayFromHeaders(response.headers);","        if (response.status === 429) admission?.penalize({ retryAfterMs: serverHintMs ?? undefined });","        lastErr = error;","      } catch (error) {","        signal?.throwIfAborted();","        if (","          error instanceof Error &&","          (/^openai_embed_failed_4(?!29)/.test(error.message) || /^openai_embed_quota_exhausted/.test(error.message))","        ) throw error;","        lastErr = error instanceof Error ? * Locate the BUNDLED node runtime as an absolute path.\n *\n * ## Why an absolute path is required, not a bare `node`\n *\n * A packaged install is not guaranteed to have node on PATH *at all*. The app\n * ships its own runtime — on macOS at\n * `/Applications/Papercusp GUI.app/Contents/Resources/sidecar/bin/node` — and\n * `main.rs` exports that directory as `PAPERCUSP_SIDECAR_BIN` for every\n * packaged/dev-sidecar boot. Any code that hands a bare `node` to a\n * *grandchild* process (a spawn whose PATH we do not control — notably git's\n * `ext::` transport command) therefore works on the dev box and fails on a real\n * install. Resolve the binary here and pass the absolute path instead.\n *\n * ## Why `process.execPath` is gated behind a basename check (EI-2114)\n *\n * In a PACKAGED Tauri build `process.execPath` is the Tauri/sidecar binary, not\n * node — handing it to something that expects a node interpreter breaks exactly\n * the installs this resolver exists to serve. It is only a valid candidate when\n * it really is a node binary. The basename filters candidates; an interpreter\n * probe establishes identity, including when a Bun executable is named node.\n *\n * The returned binary is always a plain node (never a tsx/loader wrapper), so a\n * caller whose stdout is a binary protocol can rely on it booting quietly —\n * provided the caller also strips `NODE_OPTIONS` from the child env.\n */\nimport { execFile } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { createHash } from 'node:crypto';\nimport { access, readFile, realpath } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport * as path from 'node:path';\nimport { promisify } from 'node:util';\n\nasync function pathExists(p: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    await access(p);\n    return true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * The BUNDLED node, resolved cwd-independently. `null` when this process can't\n * see it (an extracted dev-source operator whose env lacks\n * PAPERCUSP_SIDECAR_BIN and whose execPath is a tsx wrapper, not plain `node`).\n */\nexport async function resolveBundledNode(): Promise<string | null> {\n  const sidecarBin = process.env.PAPERCUSP_SIDECAR_BIN;\n  const execBase = path.basename(process.execPath).toLowerCase();\n  const execIsNode = execBase === 'node' || execBase === 'node.exe';\n  const bundledNames = process.platform === 'win32' ? ['node.exe', 'node'] : ['node', 'node.exe'];\n  const candidates = [\n    ...(sidecarBin ? bundledNames.map(name => path.join(sidecarBin, name)) : []),\n    execIsNode ? process.execPath : '',\n  ].filter(Boolean).map(candidate => path.resolve(candidate));\n  for (const c of candidates) {\n    if (!(await pathExists(c))) continue;\n    try {\n      if ((await measureRuntimePin(c)).name === 'node') return c;\n    } catch { /* Unusable or unidentified runtimes never become a Node boundary. */ }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport type ApplicationRuntimeName = 'node' | 'bun';\n\nexport interface ApplicationRuntimePin {\n  name: ApplicationRuntimeName;\n  executable: string;\n  binarySha256: string;\n  version: string;\n  platform: string;\n  arch: string;\n}\n\nexport interface ApplicationRuntimeCandidate {\n  candidateSha256: string;\n  sourceSha: string;\n  roles: string[];\n  entrypoint: string;\n  entrypointSha256: string;\n  /** Package-attested bootstrap code; [])] };\n  const runtimeArgs = [...(request.runtimeArgs ?? [])];\n  const args = [...(request.args ?? [])];\n  const env = { ...(request.env ?? process.env) };\n  if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/.test(role)\n    || !['node', 'bun'].includes(runtime)\n    || !['operational', 'isolated-candidate', 'rollback'].includes(purpose)) {\n    throw new Error('Invalid application runtime selection');\n  }\n  if (!SHA256.test(candidate.candidateSha256) || !/^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$/.test(candidate.sourceSha)\n    || !path.isAbsolute(candidate.entrypoint) || !SHA256.test(candidate.entrypointSha256)) {\n    throw new Error('An explicit pinned application artifact is required');\n  }\n  if (candidate.preload && (!path.isAbsolute(candidate.preload.path)\n    || !SHA256.test(candidate.preload.sha256))) {\n    throw new Error('An explicit pinned application preload is required');\n  }\n  if (!candidate.roles.length || candidate.roles.some(value => !/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/.test(value))\n    || new Set(candidate.roles).size !== candidate.roles.length || !candidate.roles.includes(role)) {\n    throw new Error('Runtime role is absent from the candidate role census');\n  }\n  assertRuntimePin(candidate.node, 'node');\n  if (purpose === 'rollback' && runtime !== 'node') throw new Error('Rollback selects retained Node');\n  if (runtime === 'bun') assertRuntimePin(candidate.bun!, 'bun');\n  const allowedArgs = runtime === 'node' ? => u === uid"]}],"parseDiagnostics":[],"source":"/**\n * Shared managed-child-process primitives (WI-3079, plan\n * clear-papercusp-backlog-2026-07-02).\n *\n * Extracted from the THIRD near-identical clone of the sidecar spawn +\n * respawn-with-backoff + graceful-stop shape:\n *   - sync/hyperbee/substrate-sidecar-spawn.ts   (scope-isolated, IPC handle transfer)\n *   - fleet/spawner-sidecar-spawn.ts             (simplest — but it DOES respawn)\n *   - inference-gateway/gateway-sidecar-spawn.ts (port-probe adoption, no scope isolation)\n *   - memory/embed-sidecar-spawn.ts              (also consumes these primitives)\n *\n * WHY these live here rather than being fully merged into one \"ManagedSidecar\"\n * orchestrator class: the three spawners genuinely differ in ways that are not\n * accidental duplication — substrate needs systemd-scope isolation + a raw-socket\n * IPC handle-transfer channel + a self-recursive scope-failure fallback; gateway\n * needs a TCP-listening ADOPTION probe (don't double-bind an externally-owned\n * port) + a continuous exit-driven respawn loop with re-probe-before-respawn;\n * spawner is the simplest orchestration of the set — but it DOES respawn\n * (MAX_RESPAWN_ATTEMPTS=5 over a 5-min sliding window with a give-up circuit\n * breaker, spawner-sidecar-spawn.ts:53-87). This line claimed \"no\n * respawn/circuit-breaker at all\" until 2026-08-04; it was false, and it was\n * read and believed, producing a wrong risk assessment of the bg-host cgroup\n * work (EI-19479764372783341). Forcing all of that into one\n * mega-config would trade three readable, independently-testable modules for one\n * harder-to-verify one, for a subsystem where a regression means a fleet-wide\n * agent-spawn / sync / inference outage. So this module extracts exactly the\n * pieces that ARE byte-identical (or identical-up-to-a-label) across all three —\n * the bundled-vs-dev spawn-plan resolution, the backoff + sliding-window\n * circuit-breaker bookkeeping, the SIGTERM→timeout→SIGKILL graceful-stop\n * sequence, and SIGTERM/SIGINT(+uncaughtException) shutdown-hook registration —\n * and leaves the top-level spawn/readiness/respawn orchestration local to each\n * file, where the genuine behavioral differences live. A further full-merge\n * remains a possible (riskier) follow-up if duplication resurfaces.\n */\n\nimport { readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { createRequire } from 'node:module';\nimport { dirname, join } from 'node:path';\n\n// ── bundled-vs-dev-tsx spawn-plan resolution ────────────────────────────────\n// esbuild bundles EVERY module into one serve.mjs; in a PACKAGED build\n// `import.meta.url` for the spawn module IS the bundle (no tsx, no .ts on\n// disk), so we re-exec that same artifact under the bundled node with a\n// divert env var the entrypoint checks for. In DEV (tsx, real source tree)\n// we run the sibling .ts entry under the explicit Node with the maintained tsx loader. Identical across all three\n// spawners modulo the divert env var name and the dev entry path.\n\nexport interface SidecarSpawnPlan {\n  cmd: string;\n  args: string[];\n  /** Extra env entries to layer over process.env for the child. */\n  env: Record<string, string>;\n  mode: 'bundled-reexec' | 'dev-tsx';\n}\n\nexport interface ResolveSidecarSpawnPlanOpts {\n  /** This spawn module's own resolved file — `fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)`\n   *  of the CALLER, not of this shared module. Bundled iff it does NOT end in\n   *  `.ts`/`.tsx` (esbuild emits `.mjs`/`.js`). */\n  selfPath: string;\n  /** Absolute path to the dev-mode `.ts` entry point (the sidecar script). */\n  devScriptPath: string;\n  /** Env var name the bundled re-exec sets to '1' so the entrypoint diverts\n   *  into sidecar-server mode instead of the normal operator boot path. */\n  bundledModeEnvVar: string;\n  /** Explicit retained Node executable; a Bun parent must resolve its Node boundary. */\n  execPath: string;\n  /** THIS process's own pid (`process.pid`) — declared to the child as its\n   *  parent identity. Required, not optional: a spawner that forgets to declare\n   *  itself silently reintroduces EI-20493378309289396 (see below), and a type\n   *  error at every call site is the only thing that reliably prevents that. */\n  spawnerPid: number;\n  /** Optional maintained sibling bundle. Older installs keep re-execing self. */\n  bundledScriptName?: string;\n}\n\n/** Pure: decide HOW to spawn a sidecar child (bundled re-exec-self vs dev\n *  explicit Node + tsx <entry>). Exported for unit tests. Identical decision across all\n *  three sidecar spawners, modulo the divert env var name + dev entry path. */\nexport function resolveSidecarSpawnPlan(opts: ResolveSidecarSpawnPlanOpts): SidecarSpawnPlan {\n  const isBundled = !opts.selfPath.endsWith('.ts') && !opts.selfPath.endsWith('.tsx');\n  if (isBundled) {\n    let entry = opts.selfPath;\n    if (opts.bundledScriptName) {\n      const candidate = join(dirname(opts.selfPath), opts.bundledScriptName);\n      try {\n        const info = statSync(candidate);\n        if (info.isFile() && info.size > 0) entry = candidate;\n      } catch {\n        // Older installations have no sibling; preserve the full-entry divert.\n      }\n    }\n    return {\n      cmd: opts.execPath, // the bundled node (e.g. ./bin/node), re-exec'ing selfPath\n      args: [entry],\n      env: { [opts.bundledModeEnvVar]: '1', ...parentIdentityEnv(opts.spawnerPid) },\n      mode: 'bundled-reexec',\n    };\n  }\n  const isTs = opts.devScriptPath.endsWith('.ts');\n  return {\n    cmd: opts.execPath,\n    args: isTs ? ['--import', createRequire(import.meta.url).resolve('tsx'), opts.devScriptPath] : [opts.devScriptPath],\n    env: { ...parentIdentityEnv(opts.spawnerPid) },\n    mode: 'dev-tsx',\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * (EI-20493378309289396) Re-declare THIS process as the child's parent, so the\n * child's parent-death watch checks the process that actually spawned it.\n *\n * `PAPERCUSP_DESKTOP_PARENT_PID` is set by main.rs's `spawn_serve` to the DESKTOP\n * pid, and every sidecar spawn layers `plan.env` over `...process.env` — so\n * without this the value is INHERITED and names our GRANDparent.
